				mux IMUX_CE[0] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[2] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[2] | X0_S[0] | X0_S[2] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[2] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[1] | X2_S2[1] | X2_N1[1] | X2_N2[1] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[1] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[2] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[2] | X0_S[0] | X0_S[2] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[2] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[1] | X2_S2[1] | X2_N1[1] | X2_N2[1] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[2] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[2] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[2] | X0_S[0] | X0_S[2] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[2] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[1] | X2_S2[1] | X2_N1[1] | X2_N2[1] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[3] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[2] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[2] | X0_S[0] | X0_S[2] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[2] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[1] | X2_S2[1] | X2_N1[1] | X2_N2[1] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
			}
